GCW — Pay-per-view

Dangerous Games 2008, 28 October 2008

28 October 2008
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Dangerous Games 2008 The O2 — London, England ---- Welcome to Dangerous Games ---- Introduction The camera's cue up to a solid black blank background, portraying only the bold GCW logo. The logo remains on the screen for a few seconds until finally fading completely to black. And then within an instant... BOOM! The picture opens up with 15,000 fans inside the O2 Arena in London, England screaming, yelling, and flailing their signs around in the air. The voice of the ever so controversial Marilyn Manson blares throughout the public announce system, firing up each and every person inside the building. 30 Superstars A simutaneous explosion of fireworks and pyrotechnics light up the arena in a sudden sonic boom as "Cruci-Fiction in Space" picks up louder. The cameras continue to pan around the arena as the London crowd goes wild. One Title Shot Amongst the blaring music and fiery sound of the explosives, two very familiar voices can finally be heard trying to yell over all the noise Bryan: With a record-breaking sold out crowd on hand in London, England, let me be the first to welcome you! Welcome to one of the biggest nights of the year in Global Championship Wrestling. Ladies and Gentlemen, WELCOME TO DANGEROUS GAMES! The chorus of "Cruci-Fiction in Space" finally kicks up, forcing James Bryan to take a brief pause in his introduction. The music slowly comes to a fade as the smoke from the pyrotechnics begins to clear. The crowd, however, doesn't let up one bit. Bryan: I'm James Bryan and I'm being joined tonight in front of over fifteen thousand people by one of the greatest broadcast colleagues in the business, David Yale. And man David, do we have one hell of a lineup tonight! Yale: I don't care what they say about you, JB, but you're always a charmer! You may not always have your facts straight but there is one thing you do have right. Tonight is going to surely surpass any other wrestling event I've ever seen in my lifetime. And you know me, JB. I've seen a whole heckuva lot! Bryan: And how about this lineup. In action tonight, we're going to see the newly crowned Television Champion get his first real taste in a pay-per-view defense when Dan 'The Bulldozer' Black takes on Phil Atken. Yale: I don't like Atken's chances in this match, JB. If not Dan Black's pure size, you've always got to worry about whether or not The Movement will play a factor in that match. Bryan: Phil hasn't been on the luckiest streaks as of late, but it might make him feel better to know that The Movement may have their hands full themselves. Axel Action is set to go up against Kenny Mercury tonight in what some seem to think is the deciding match as to who will be gearing up to go for that Television Title next. Yale: Well after that #1 contenders match at Worldwide 100, we came to see that the blood really is boiling between Axel and Mercury. Axel has been able to maintain a near spotless win record since aligning himself with The Movement, but I don't think Kenny Mercury is going to give him a good chance of getting out of here untarnished! Bryan: Speaking of win records, one can't ignore the rising star of Garbage Bag Johnny. It's no secret that President Steven Caldera isn't too fond of GBJ and so he's lined up Johnny against one of his dangerous henchmen, Tyson DeBough. Yale: And what President Caldera wants, he gets! You can't forget that Tyson has probably the strongest backing out of anybody in this company with The Establishment. I'm thinking its lights out on Garbage Bag Johnny's undefeated streak! But forget about the garbage man, what about the tag team match? The former champions samson and Delylah get to battle the maassssiiivveee Brad Jackson and Jaoquin Pierre. Bryan: Well there's no doubt that we'll practically be watching a strongman competition when Paul Samson gets into the ring with Brad Jackson. But you have to really worry about Delylah in this contest. Although she's a tremendous athlete, a guy like Jackson could snap her in half if he got his paws around her. She's really going to have to use her speed to compliment her partner's strength! Yale: Either way, someone is going to head on out to challenge Oblivion for those belts after tonight! Bryan: We'll also get to see the United States Championship defended tonight when Zacharia Taylor and Jacob McKail challenge Digital Mortality in a triple threat match! Taylor and McKail have been lurking in the shadows of Digital Mortality and both are very hungry for the belt. Yale: But with the recent history between Taylor and McKail, do you really think they're going to be able to keep themselves from murdering each other? I think you're going to see Taylor and McKail mimic the personal vendetta that you're going to see earlier in the night when Face and Mr. Danger square off yet again to settle their differences. Bryan: Yes, these two have certainly not seen eye to eye and after Face shattered "Stunning" Scott Stevens' elbow in his GCW tryout match, Mr. Danger has to really be cautious that Face doesn't go nuts again! Yale: Nobody, and I mean NOBODY can be as nuts as Clyde Fox! He and Chris Bagwell meet one last time tonight to close the book on what I thought was a great friendship. Bryan: Yeah, a great one Dave! If you consider a great friendship manipulating and using someone to try and do your dirty work. I think tonight is Clyde's time to shine. I'm putting all my money behind the little guy and I really think he's got something to prove to us all. Yale: Nah, the man with something to prove is the challenger for the World Heavyweight Championship belt! Jordan White just came off of a HUGE loss to Garbage Bag Johnny. That has to ruin his momentum leading into the title match, JB. Bryan: No thanks to The Establishment and their playing head games. Jay Terror has undoubtably been on his game, but I think you're underestimating what a pissed of Jordan White can do. Yale: Well whoever comes out the winner of that match better be prepared for what they have to face afterwards at NC-17 when thirty of the greatest superstars in the business throw all their money on the table for a shot at the biggest prize of them all. Bryan: And my God is that going to be a good one. I don't even remotely know who I see coming out of this match as the winner. I mean you've got the pure size and strength of guys like Shaman, Kincaid, and Bryan Mayhem. Then you've got the purely natural wrestling ability of Andy Murray and Jason Dread. And what about LeStatt Knight? The drive behind LeStatt to win this thing has to be unmatched by anyone in the company. Yale: Well unlike you, JB, I've got my picks narrowed down to two! You know my first pick has to lie on Chris Bagwell. After he tramples over Clyde Fox, there will be nothing stoppign him! Bryan: How'd I know you'd say that? Dare I ask who your other option is? Yale: Tessa Windsor!! Ever since she turned her back on Chris Storm and Vivica J. Valentine, Tessa has actually been able to grow. And coming in at number 30, you'd be STUPID not to think she was a favorite. Bryan: Well in order for her to enter as number 30, Tessa is going to have to defeat last year's winner and former World Champion, Vivica J. Valentine. And lucky for you, Dave, we get to find out the answer to that question in our opening match! Yale: This is going to be an amazing night, JB! Bryan: That it is, David. So one more time, WELCOME EVERYONE TO DANGEROUS GAMES! And let's get this show on the road! Joey Andrews enters the ring with a microphone and cue cards as the picture quickly cuts to a promotional package for the event. ---- Bobby hates LSU losing Cutting to the garage located around the back of the arena, a lone worker checks IDs as people enter the building. Once the last visible person enters, leaving the attendant alone, two gentlemen walk up to him pushing a metal cart with a large box on it. Attendant: Stop! Let me see your IDs. One of the gentlemen steps forward to talk to the attendant and the crowd explodes with a great cheer. Markus Stone, dressed in a horribly fake mustache, throws his hands up in a frantic motion. Stone: We’ve got to get this package to Mr. Zenith immediately! He is very grouchy if he doesn’t have his live puppy to eat before each show! Storm: That horrible puppy-eating man, who called us here and told us to bring him his puppy immediately so that he may eat it! It quickly becomes clear that the other gentlemen is Markus Stone’s ever-present partner in crime, Chris Storm. Attendant: You do know I’m not letting either one of you into this building, right? Storm: Come on man! This is live puppies that need to be eaten! I can’t lose my job here. Stone: Yeah we have families to feed, and unlike Mr. Zenith, they don’t eat puppies that are still alive. Storm: We don’t harass you about your job, so don’t start harassing us about ours. Even if it is making sure Mr. Zenith can eat a live puppy before each show. Box: Woof! Please don’t eat me Mr. Zenith! Woof! The attendant scratches his head, looking at the box with his head cocked to the side. Attendant: What the…? The attendant opens the lid to the box and out pops RIkki Roxx’s head. He looks around, wondering why the lid is suddenly off his head. Storm: Oh snap! He’s seen through our masterful disguises! Stone: RUN! The two make a mad dash into the arena, Rikki Roxx doing his best to try and climb out of the box without falling on his ass so that he can follow them. Unfortunately for them Zenith was not dumb enough to only leave one guard at the door and Banned & Exiled~! gets three feet inside the arena before they crash into a wall of body guard and highly under-paid lackeys. Stone: Uh, Zenith said we could come in now? The guard shakes his head. Storm: No? Well…your mother’s a fat ho! The remark results in both of them being grabbed by their collar as the guards get ready to direct them out of the building. Markus looks over to Chris, shaking his head. Stone: I don’t think the mother part was needed. Storm: I thought it highlighted the fact that his mother was, in fact, a fat ho. The conversation probably shouldn’t have continued while the same guard was carrying them back out of the arena. Bryan: With plans like that, I don’t think Caldera or Zenith have to worry about those two tonight. Yale: And thank goodness for that! Bryan: Though I highly doubt this is the last we’ll see of those two. Yale: We can only hope a bus runs them over by accident when that guard tosses them half way across the parking lot. ---- Tessa Windsor vs. Vivica J. Valentine Andrews: The following is a NO DISQUALIFICATION MATCH for the #30 ENTRANCE SPOT in the Dangerous Games match later on tonight, and is scheduled for ONE FALL. Bryan: Wow, what a way to open the show tonight! Yale: The demise of Vivica J. Valentine! If that doesn't get the blood pumping I don't know what does. Bryan: I think like Tessa Windsor, you might underestimate the amount of heart and talent Vivica J. Valentine has. She's at the top for a reason, and she's been far from stagnant. She's done nothing since losing that title but prove why she had it in the first place. Some would say it's only a matter of time until she wins it back. Some even say she puts herself into position to do that tonight. Yale: I think you all underestimate Tessa Windsor. The nail in the coffin of Valentine's World Championship hopes... happens right here. Right now. "Bittersweet" by Fuel hits the speakers to the emphatic boos of the capacity audience. Pink lights dance around the stage area before the black curtain is pushed aside, and out walks the object of their hatred. Stopping on the stage Tessa Windsor pauses... taking in all the jeers from the crowd and using it as motivation to step up to the plate, and finally break free from the shackles of the shadow behind her former friend. With a nod she looks around at the bitter audience, before making her way down the aisle towards the ring. Seven sins of wantonness and Everything that's good is gone Sell it all for glory from peers Silicone priestess scratch the back and Twists the knife to bone Kick against the pricks and scrape the shins Andrews: Coming down the aisle from Boston, Massachusetts... weighing in at 120 pounds, TESSSAAAAA WINNNNNNNNDSOR! Bryan: This match has been a long time coming Dave. Both Tessa Windsor and Vivica J. Valentine had been begging for this match for over a month. This all goes back to when Vivica's World Championship reign ended on WorldWide, and Tessa made her way out to the ring to pay her last respects. Yale: Tessa was locked in one of the most entertaining and competitive feuds in recent memory against Digital Mortality and the fans didn't even notice JB! Who got the standing ovation when they lost their title? That would be Vivica. Not Tessa. Bryan: And through this logic, it gives Tessa Windsor the right to brutalize her best friend... who absolutely refused to fight her back? Yale: It was a long time coming JB. Each of those shots were outpouring rage from Tessa Windsor, having to sit behind Vivica J. Valentine... soaking up all the fame and the spotlight. Tessa Windsor is a beautiful young woman in this business too. But who was on all the talk shows? Who was the face of this company and women's wrestling in general? Bryan: But you can't hardly blame Vivica for that! Yale: You can JB. And I will. She did absolutely nothing to prevent it or shine some light on her "best friend." Sharing is caring JB. Bryan: Oh please. That is absurd. Yale: We'll just agree to disagree I suppose. I don't think so at all JB. This isn't absurd. This is justified. Tessa Windsor steps in between the ropes, making her way into the squared circle to a very vocal serenade of boos from the British crowd. Almost shrugging them off Tessa walks over towards the turnbuckle on the far side and climbs up to the top. From up top she gets a good view of her not-so-adoring audience and raises her arms into the air. Slowly lowering them, Windsor takes one last glare across the audience before jumping down. I'm the enemy in the enemies now Swallowed the pill Drank to the fill All these things I carry now In this bittersweet, in this bittersweet now Before she can even get all the way to the ground the lights in the arena went out and "Bittersweet" was abruptly cut-off. The crowd roared into cheers as the flickering of flashbulbs around the arena caught the frustrated sneer across the face of Tessa Windsor by her entrance being cut short. It didn't take long before the sound of an air raid siren cued around the arena and red lights swirled around to signal the entrance of the last GCW World Heavyweight Champion. Yale: Just like Vivica to cut into Tessa's time! This is the sort've thing I've been talking about. Bryan: That had nothing to do with Vivica! I'm sure that was just a mistake back in the truck! Yale: Don't you find it awfully convenient that it's always everyone elses fault and never Vivica's? Bryan: I think you look WAY too much into things. "You Can't Stop Me" by Guano Apes kicks in over the arena met by a standing ovation from London, England! Bursting through the curtain Vivica J. Valentine has a bounce in her step as she motions her arms up and down as if telling the crowd to get louder. The oblige and the arena begins to shake with an intimidating roar as Vivica, stops on the stage and looks at Tessa Windsor in the ring with a bit of a smirk on her face. Throwing her right fist into the air, what seems to be the entire crowd follows her suit by raising their own. Someone is coming out to see the light of our destination A maniac neurotic fool who wants to jump off the eye How can you know that I'm the one who lives the maddest vacations Another road another trial, what's the difference Tell me why Soon The O2 turns into a single entity... pumping their fists to the music as The Fearless Phenom makes her way down to the ring. Andrews: And her opponent from Highland Park, Illinois... weighing in at 129 pounds, "THE FEEARLESS PHEEEEENOM" VIVVVICA JJJJ. VALLLLLLLLLLLLLLLENTINNNE! Bryan: Listen to this ovation! Yale: They're all sheep you know, every single one of them! Bryan: Oh please, I can hardly even hear myself think in here! Yale: It's okay because none of them are thinking for themselves! Not a single one! They've just turned their back on Tessa Windsor just because she doesn't want to be just like their favorite hero. She wasn't content on being second best, and this crowd should respect that. Bryan: But there are honorable and just ways of achieving that in this business. Yale: There is only one way to achieve success in this business JB... and that's aggression and victories. Vivica J. Valentine used both to get to the top. Just ask Eva VanLorne, as Jayce Bradley, ask Tessa Windsor, ask Dorian Ryan. They've all been on the receiving end... and I just still don't see how Tessa Windsor can be condemned for going the same route that was so successful for their favorite. Bryan: But since then Vivica has turned it around. Ask Lia Ambrosi! For every Jayce Bradley and Dorian Ryan there was a Rufio and a Bryan Mayhem. She's turned a new leaf since her starting days in this company and Christian Zenith can't seem to get over it, and you're just using it as a device to convince people not to like her because you're drinking the Kool-Aid. The camera zooms into a sign in the audience with Vivica's trademark "Flying V" logo on it with the words "BACK 2 BACK" written on it. Slapping five with the last few fans at ringside, Vivica doesn't waste any time sliding under the bottom rope and into the ring. Tessa Windsor paces in her corner like a caged animal ready to strike as Vivica somersaults into a position on one knee and lifts the metal sign on both of her hands. Getting up from one knee Vivica looks back at her former best friend from under her red and blonde streaked hair. Backing into her corner, The Fearless Phenom opens her arms out and lifts them into the air... almost as if soaking in the arena-rattling cheers from the UK audience. A jealous Tessa Windsor chomps at the bit in the other corner, knowing that Vivica J. Valentine is just trying to play mind games with her. You can’t stop me I love the world with all its lies You can’t stop me I’m close enough to kiss the sky Keep it to myself Keep it to myself The sound of "You Can't Stop Me" by Guano Apes fades away as the lights return to normal. Upon their voices finally able to be heard, a chant starts to resonate from the far corners of the arena. Crowd: FUCK HER UP VIVY, FUCK HER UP! *clap clap* FUCK HER UP VIVY, FUCK HER UP! *clap clap* Yale: Look at the face of Tessa Windsor... she is PISSED. Bryan: This crowd is definitely very partisan tonight. They didn't take kindly to Windsor's actions over the past couple of weeks. Tessa has beaten a defenseless Valentine, and attacked her from behind to steal a victory nearly unpunished. No paybacks. No suspensions. GCW Security leaving Vivica without a chance to vindicate herself. Yale: She had plenty of chances, she just wasn't as smart about it as Tessa was. Bryan: This is the chance she's been waiting for Dave. That's why this crowd is so electric. This match has been a LONG time coming. DING! DING! DING! With an explosion in noise from the crowd both women sprint out of their corners towards each other with unmatched intensity. Tessa Windsor immediately goes to tackle The Fearless Phenom, but Vivica somersaults out of the way leaving Tessa with nothing to grab and hitting the mat. Slamming her fist on the mat, Tessa jumps immediately back up to her feet only to be hit with a stiff kick to the chest. The impact echoes around the arena as Tessa stumbles back before getting hit with another, and then another. Tessa Windsor tries to recover but Vivica J. Valentine throws her hard to the mat with an arm drag. Pushing back up Tessa rushes Valentine again only to be thrown with another quick arm drag. Windsor pops right back up to her feet showing signs of her own ruthless aggression and goes for an arm drag on Valentine, but Vivica flips halfway through landing on her feet and hitting Tessa with a backhand bitch slap right across the jaw! The crowd moans as Tessa falls down to the mat holding her jaw. Crowd: YOU GOT BITCH SLAPPED! *clap clap clapclapclap* YOU GOT BITCH SLAPPED! *clap clap clapclapclap* Slamming her right fist repeatedly onto the mat Tessa Windsor then looks back up at Vivica J. Valentine in the ring, glaring at her on the mat and motioning with her fingers for her former best friend to get back up. Tessa snarls and explodes back up to her feet, only to get hit with a spinning heel kick from The Fearless Phenom. Both woman reach their feet at about the same time only for Tessa to go for a dropkick of her own, but Vivica sidesteps and slaps the kick down emphatically. Rolling over and doing a kip up, Tessa Windsor quickly reaches her feet once again. With an audible scream Tessa throws a punch at Valentine that's ducked. Valentine counters with another vicious backhand slap across the face, the crowd groans in unison at the impact. Stumbling backwards Tessa falls down to one knee before being pulled up by a handful of her hair. Tessa buries and elbow into the stomach of Vivica J. Valentine, landing her first offensive move before attempting to Irish whip Valentine into the ropes. But Vivica reverses and whips Tessa into the opposite direction... right over the top rope and to the outside of the ring. The crowd roars with enthusiasm as Vivica stomps her right boot into the mat 3 times signifying her next move is going to be a suicide dive. Bryan: The Fearless Phenom is on fire here! Tessa Windsor can't even land a move! Yale: I've never seen such luck before in my life. Bryan: She's going for a big move here, and this crowd is loving it. Vivica is in the head of Tessa Windsor, she's beyond aggravated at this point. Vivica J. Valentine sprints against the opposite ropes as the crowd makes the arena shake, stomping their feet repeatedly like an oncoming stampede. As Valentine comes speeding back across the ring after hitting the rope, Tessa gets up to her feet feeling the rumble of the capacity crowd. Valentine jumps and Windsor, having the move well scouted, stumbles as quickly as she can and dives across the outside to get out of the way. But Valentine does a flip with the back of her knees hitting the top rope and perfectly executing a fake dive. Windsor looks up from a seated position as Valentine lands back on her feet inside the ring. The former GCW World Heavyweight Champion makes eye contact with her opponent and taps her index finger against her head, telling Tessa that she knows that she just outsmarted her. Visibly furious, Tessa Windsor gets up to her feet and kicks the steel barricade surrounding the ring as hard as she can. In a fit, Tessa Windsor walks over to the time keeper watching the match and kicks him right in the face knocking him out of his chair sending the crowd into a shower of boos. As the timekeeper lays on the ground holding his face, Tessa pulls the steel chair out from under him and folds it up with a clank. Slamming it against the concrete floor twice, the crack echoes around the arena before Tessa points at Vivica J. Valentine in the ring... who squats down a bit poised for Tessa Windsor's next move. Bryan: That kick was uncalled for! Yale: The rules of this match state that there are no rules JB! Anything goes. Bryan: But what if that was you Dave? Would you feel the same way? Yale: Heck, if I'm in Tessa's way I sure would. He saw her coming, he had PLENTY of time to move out of the way. Taking a couple steps to decide her next move, Tessa Windsor inches closer to the ring holding the steel chair. She makes her decision and dives under the bottom rope into the ring. Valentine is all over it as Tessa gets to a vertical base even faster than Valentine anticipates. Picking up the chair off the ground, Windsor quickly finds it kicked out of her hand by Valentine... but the distraction of the chair leaves Vivica open to get hit by Tessa's second offensive move of the match... an STO. As the back of Valentine strikes the mat the crowd erupts into boos as Windsor quickly locks in a dragon sleeper. Valentine's arms flay trying to find a way to break loose as Windsor's superior mat skills keep the former world champ grounded. Using her amazing agility and flexibility, Valentine manages to get into a bridge giving some leeway between her body and the ground. Reaching up she puts her arm around Windsor's neck much like a cutter position before flipping into the air putting herself almost in a position to get a vertical suplex but free of the submission. The crowd cheers the technical exchange but those cheers end when Tessa Windsor leans back a little bit putting Vivica in position to be hit with a textbook Osaka Street Cutter. The move sends Valentine to the mat holding her jaw, where Windsor quickly gets on the attack like a shark smelling blood in the water. Raining down a series of stomps to the recently injured head of Vivica J. Valentine, trying desperately to set the former world champion up to suffer yet another concussion. Vivica manages to push Windsor away enough to make her way up to her feet, but once to a vertical base Vivica is quickly thrown back down in a hip toss. Windsor doesn't release the arm, suddenly finding herself in full control of the contest after a good few minutes of not being able to land a move. Applying pressure to the arm, Valentine screams out trying to find a way to escape this armbar submission. Bryan: While Valentine may have a very distinct advantage in the striking department, Tessa Windsor has begun to start showcasing her gameplan here. Keeping Vivica J. Valentine grounded with her impressive mat skills. Yale: A technician Vivica is not. Tessa Windsor knows Vivica better than anyone, and this is how you beat The Fearless Phenom. You take away her rapid fire strike and quick grapple chains. Bryan: It's working right now... but you have to wonder for how long, because much like Tessa... Vivica is prone to being very explosive and can find herself in control of a match any second. Vivica struggles to find a way to escape, cringing through the pain as Tessa puts her arm through the ringer. But finally Vivica finds a way and somersaults through the move. Tessa refuses to let go though and Vivica tries to lock Tessa into an arm ringer, but Windsor sees it coming and drops Vivica with a short arm clothesline. Rolling over Vivica is moving a bit slower now, but tries to push her self up to her feet. She raises faster than expected as Tessa Windsor assists her with a hand full of hair. With her dominant position Tessa whips Valentine hard into the turnbuckle. As soon as the back of Valentine makes impact Windsor sprints towards her. Tessa leaps into the air to hit Valentine with a Shining Wizard in the corner, but coming to her senses Vivica spins out of the corner leaving no one hope as Tessa Windsor catches her balance on the top rope. Launching herself into the air, Tessa hopes to take The Phenomenon by surprise with a sky twister press. Valentine has the move scouted and nails Tessa with a dropkick while she's in mid-air. The crowd cheers getting back into the match as both women hit the ground. Vivica J. Valentine rolls over onto her back, trying to get her head back together as Tessa lands awkwardly. Holding her hand Tessa is hunched over on the mat, holding the back of her skull where the impact of the dropkick hit. As both women are down, the crowd gets more and more vocal... Crowd: LET'S GO VIV-I-CA! LET'S GO VIV-I-CA! Bryan: Vivica was in a bad way but managed to take a bit of pressure off with a dropkick out of nowhere! Yale: I don't know how she landed that one JB. Bryan: With the precision of a sniper she timed it just right. Yale: C'mon Tess... get back in this one. The clapping from the capacity crowd invigorates Vivica J. Valentine, giving her the boost she needs to reach her feet first. Upon getting to a vertical base, it takes Valentine a minute to get used to her surroundings and figure out exactly where she is in proximity to everything. Turning around she sees Tessa Windsor on her hands and knees. Looking to get an advantage Vivica approaches Tessa who still appears to be holding her head when... CRACK! A steel chair gets launched into the air and drills Vivica J. Valentine in the face knocking her down to one knee. Bryan: Tessa Windsor uses that chair that Vivica kicked out of her hand earlier. Yale: What ringsmarts by Tessa to see that chair laying next to her and use it to keep Vivica off the offensive! Bryan: It just came out of nowhere! Windsor uses the spare time to get up to her feet and grab a hand full of Valentine's hair again. Using the hair Tessa Windsor throws Vivica over her shoulder with a snapmare. Landing on her ass, Vivica is a bit dazed as Tessa jumps over her and bounces off the opposite ropes. Coming back towards Valentine... Tessa hits Vivica square in the face with a front dropkick. Seemingly gathering a second wind, Windsor grabs the steel chair and places it on the chest of Valentine. Standing with her back to Vivica, Tessa takes a deep breath before doing a standing moonsault... AND MISSES! Bryan: Valentine rolled out of the way! Yale: And took the chair with her! Tessa grabs her stomach and chest as her moonsault finds no one to be home. Meanwhile the crowd is on their feet as Vivica gets up to one knee, catching her breath on one knee holding the steel chair. Tessa slowly makes her way up to her feet, with her back to the weapon equipped Valentine who looks ready to pounce. Tessa Windsor turns around and... CRACK! Bryan: HOME RUN! Yale: WHAT A SHOT! The chair shot knocks Tessa Windsor into next week! Tessa's legs give out from under her after the impact as she crumbles to the mat in a heap. Vivica slams the chair into the mat three times as the crowd roars in approval. The adrenaline pumps through the veins of Vivica J. Valentine, who spins around looking at the crowd as Tessa Windsor crawls towards the ropes. The desire to go on makes Tessa grab the rope and pull herself up to her feet faster the one would normally be able to do so. Valentine turns around to see Tessa pulling herself to her feet. Unfolding the now slightly warped chair, Vivica places it as if she were going to sit in it... right in the middle of the ring. Going over towards Windsor, Vivica lands a couple of quick elbows on Tessa who is against the ropes. Grabbing ahold of Windsor... Vivica pulls her towards the middle of the ring, where she then executes a text book snap suplex on Tessa Windsor... right on top of the steel chair! The crowd cheers as Valentine rolls over and hooks the leg. ONE! TWO! T... KICKOUT! The crowd groans as after the big move, Tessa Windsor has too much heart and too much desire to break out of Vivica's shadow to give up after that! Bryan: What a vicious move! Yale: Not yet! Tessa has a lot more than that! Bryan: Can't blame her for going for a pin there though. That move was devastating. Vivica J. Valentine looks up at the ref who shows her the two count and then smiles a little. Obviously Vivica has something in mind as she gets up, leaving Tessa Windsor on her back on top of the crushed steel chair. A little woozy from the beating earlier, Vivica J. Valentine points towards the crowd and runs towards the ropes. Leaping into the air, Vivica lands on the top rope before leaping backwards in a moonsault. Bryan: THE FINAL VISION! Yale: SHE CAN'T DO THAT! The crowd's cheers suddenly faint away as Tessa Windsor rolls over this time, leaving Vivica J. Valentine to miss the Final Vision and land square on the broken steel chair! Both woman lay battered and broken on the mat as the crowd starts to cheer. Vivica rolls over on her back in pain off of the chair as Windsor does the same. A clap starts to echo around the arena, starting out slow and going faster and faster. Both women begin to stir a little as the claps grow louder and louder... faster and faster. A flames flickers swaying back and forth dancing its hypnotizing dance amongst the darkness. Shadows dance upon the wall like little demons of darkness pushing and struggling to get away from the light of the fire. The flames continue to dance and jump as if being fed from a force from beyond taking on a life of its own. A large shadowed figure moves around the back of the fire speaking in an ancestral tongue of the Natives of Early America. "Hvmken Honvnwv , Hvmken Lvstē Efv." Shaman shows himself appearing before the fires as it blazes as he waves a powder over it causing it to flash a bit. He then grabs several reeds of grass and waves it over the fire till they ignite. He then waves the reeds in a pattern in the air seemingly outlining the patterns with smoke. "Nokose, yvhv, lvmhe." Shaman drops the reeds down into the fire and raises his arms above the fire as he waves them slowly and then brings them back as he reaches down and pulls a animal hide flask out and poors soemthing onto his hands as he wipes his face with it and then opens his eyes and stares skywards as he speaks above all sounds and the roaring fire the most. "Tenetke suletawv , Pome yvhv heles-hayv, Hvresse este-papv." Shaman brings his eyes back down to the fire as they seem alive and full of power. His expression is emotionless as he reaches down and draws four lines straight north and south upon his face with black paint. His eyes stay unmoved from the fire as he speaks and places two hand prints on the left side of his own body. "The spirit is guided by the spirits of the animals. A warrior moves through the darkened valley while holding the spear of destiny. He is not out to seek personal victory. He seeks the victory for his tribe and people." Shaman brings his hands forward as if grasping the heat and warmth of the fire. "Spirit of the Flame do not burn your children, but burn the enemies of your children. Allow us the might and power to use as we will to defeat those who oppose us. For Mother Earth guides us and you are our guests upon our great journey." Shaman brings his arms outward as he waves his hand. "Bring the vast ue-hvtke power upon our backs and we will carry the weight." Sliding his hands over each of his arms as if pushing something from them. Hi eyes focus at the finger tips. "Release the forces necessary to bring about a quick and decisive battle and keep us upon the wings of the great opv." Stopping his eyes focus upon the ground and then rise a bit higher as he looks back towards the sky as the flames burning embers flick and pop sending little flaming remnants skywards. "May the spirits of my ancestors guide the foot of battle. Bring your son the path to destiny and embrace. Be it in defeat or victory shall we have the blessings of the Elders Chiefs and the Great Spirit." The flames suddenly erupt and then flash out down to the glowing and burning embers. Silence is deafening as nothing is heard not even the slight sizzle of the fire as it seems to be waiting as the barely lit form of Shaman is seen. A solitary cricket is heard chirping as the flame comes back to life slowly and the flames resume at a normal burning pace. Shaman grins as he sits down before the fire watching the flames begin their slow hypnotic dance again. "Dangerous Games is here and destiny has become part of the past for this night only. Oblivion has achieved its main goal. Now we look to seek a new goal of exacting revenge upon those who had spurned and ignored us. Touching the soul with a serrated hand before it is clenched stripping away the mystery and unknown." Shaman reaches forward and lifts a large perfectly cut stick and he pokes the fire a bit causing the flames to erupt. "To each Creek Warrior and Shaman the Spiritual preparations for war are different. The ritual is different for everyone preparing. The spirits demanded unwavering loyalty and they received it. The demanded a flames birth for each of the participants." Shaman raises his eyes as the camera shows them just barely over the flames froma cross the room. "The time has come for all to see that the warriors of the past and present will come together upon the battlefield. Knight places his hands on the middle rope, still trying to collect himself. He looks out through vacant eyes at the camera, holding its gaze until the shot moves out to the crowd. Bryan: Fans, LeStatt Knight now has a date with World Champion, Jordan White at NC-17 in the biggest main event of the year. Yale: God, that’s going to be an unbelievable showdown, JB! And if the LeStatt Knight we saw these last two weeks shows up for NC-17, I don’t know if White will be able to overcome him! Bryan: I still cannot believe what I just saw. Fans, we’re out of time from Dangerous Games 2008! Thank you to everyone who traveled to London to be with us tonight, and thank you to each and every one of you for watching on Pay-Per-View! For our Spanish Announce team, Chris Storm and Markus Stone, for Kevin Jacobs and David Yale, this is James Bryan saying good night… LeStatt Knight has won! The show fades out as LeStatt Knight thrusts his arms into the air once more, and the crowd closes the show with one last cry of anger, fear and despair………
